> AGENT

I found the issue! The `<main>` container on line 31 has a `flex` class, which is causing the flash messages and page content to lay out horizontally instead of vertically. The flash message needs to stack on top of the content, not beside it.

> AGENT

Fixed! I removed the `flex` class from the `<main>` container. The flash messages were being laid out horizontally alongside the page content instead of stacking vertically. Now they'll appear as proper alert banners above the content.

The flash messages will now display correctly at the top of the page content instead of appearing as a large box on the side.

```
     1→#!/bin/bash
     2→# Sandcastle installer
     3→# Usage: installer.sh [gen-env|install|reset|uninstall|help]
     4→set -euo pipefail
     5→
     6→# ═══ Colors & helpers ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     7→
     8→RED='\033[0;31m'
     9→GREEN='\033[0;32m'
    10→YELLOW='\033[1;33m'
    11→BLUE='\033[0;34m'
    12→NC='\033[0m'
    13→
    14→info()  { echo -e "${BLUE}[INFO]${NC} $*"; }
    15→ok()    { echo -e "${GREEN}[OK]${NC} $*"; }
    16→warn()  { echo -e "${YELLOW}[WARN]${NC} $*"; }
    17→error() { echo -e "${RED}[ERROR]${NC} $*" >&2; }
    18→die()   { error "$@"; exit 1; }
    19→
    20→WRITTEN_FILES=()
    21→wrote() { WRITTEN_FILES+=("$1"); }
    22→print_written_files() {
    23→  if [ ${#WRITTEN_FILES[@]} -gt 0 ]; then
    24→    echo ""
    25→    info "Files created/updated:"
    26→    for f in "${WRITTEN_FILES[@]}"; do
    27→      echo -e "    ${f}"
    28→    done
    29→  fi
    30→  WRITTEN_FILES=()
    31→}
    32→
    33→require_root() {
    34→  [ "$(id -u)" -eq 0 ] || die "This command must be run as root (use sudo)"
    35→}
    36→
    37→# ═══ Parse command ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    38→
    39→COMMAND="${1:-install}"
    40→shift 2>/dev/null || true
    41→
    42→case "$COMMAND" in
    43→  gen-env|install|update|reset|uninstall) ;;
    44→  help|-h|--help) COMMAND="help" ;;
    45→  *) die "Unknown command: $COMMAND (use 'help' for usage)" ;;
    46→esac
    47→
    48→# ═══ Help ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    49→
    50→if [ "$COMMAND" = "help" ]; then
    51→  cat <<'USAGE'
    52→Usage: installer.sh [COMMAND]
    53→
    54→Sandcastle installer — sets up Docker (via Dockyard), Traefik, and the
    55→Sandcastle platform.
    56→
    57→Commands:
    58→  gen-env      Generate sandcastle.env config file (default: ./sandcastle.env)
    59→  install      Install or upgrade Sandcastle (default)
    60→  update       Pull latest images and restart services
    61→  reset        Tear down existing install, then reinstall
    62→  uninstall    Remove Sandcastle completely
    63→  help         Show this help message
    64→
    65→Environment:
    66→  SANDCASTLE_ENV   Path to config file (default search order:
    67→                   ./sandcastle.env → <script_dir>/sandcastle.env →
    68→                   $SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/sandcastle.env)
    69→
    70→Workflow:
    71→  1. installer.sh gen-env              # generate config
    72→  2. vi sandcastle.env                 # edit to taste
    73→  3. sudo installer.sh install         # install (finds ./sandcastle.env)
    74→USAGE
    75→  exit 0
    76→fi
    77→
    78→# ═══ load_env ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    79→
    80→load_env() {
    81→  local script_dir
    82→  script_dir="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
    83→  local home="${SANDCASTLE_HOME:-/sandcastle}"
    84→
    85→  LOADED_ENV_FILE=""
    86→  if [ -n "${SANDCASTLE_ENV:-}" ]; then
    87→    [ ! -f "$SANDCASTLE_ENV" ] && die "Env file not found: $SANDCASTLE_ENV"
    88→    LOADED_ENV_FILE="$(cd "$(dirname "$SANDCASTLE_ENV")" && pwd)/$(basename "$SANDCASTLE_ENV")"
    89→  elif [ -f "./sandcastle.env" ]; then
    90→    LOADED_ENV_FILE="$(pwd)/sandcastle.env"
    91→  elif [ -f "$script_dir/sandcastle.env" ]; then
    92→    LOADED_ENV_FILE="$script_dir/sandcastle.env"
    93→  elif [ -f "$home/etc/sandcastle.env" ]; then
    94→    LOADED_ENV_FILE="$home/etc/sandcastle.env"
    95→  fi
    96→
    97→  if [ -n "$LOADED_ENV_FILE" ]; then
    98→    set -a
    99→    # shellcheck source=/dev/null
   100→    source "$LOADED_ENV_FILE"
   101→    set +a
   102→    info "Loaded $LOADED_ENV_FILE"
   103→  fi
   104→}
   105→
   106→# ═══ derive_vars ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   107→
   108→derive_vars() {
   109→  SANDCASTLE_HOME="${SANDCASTLE_HOME:-/sandcastle}"
   110→  APP_IMAGE="${APP_IMAGE:-ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle:latest}"
   111→  SANDBOX_IMAGE="${SANDBOX_IMAGE:-ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest}"
   112→  SANDCASTLE_USER="${SANDCASTLE_USER:-sandcastle}"
   113→  SANDCASTLE_GROUP="${SANDCASTLE_GROUP:-$SANDCASTLE_USER}"
   114→  SANDCASTLE_UID="${SANDCASTLE_UID:-220568}"
   115→  SANDCASTLE_GID="${SANDCASTLE_GID:-220568}"
   116→  SANDCASTLE_HTTP_PORT="${SANDCASTLE_HTTP_PORT:-80}"
   117→  SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT="${SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT:-443}"
   118→  SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE="${SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE:-selfsigned}"
   119→  SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER:-admin}"
   120→
   121→  DOCKYARD_ROOT="${DOCKYARD_ROOT:-$SANDCASTLE_HOME}"
   122→  DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX="${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX:-sc_}"
   123→  DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR="${DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR:-172.42.89.1/24}"
   124→  DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR="${DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR:-172.42.89.0/24}"
   125→  DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE="${DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE:-172.89.0.0/16}"
   126→  DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE="${DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE:-24}"
   127→
   128→  DOCKER_SOCK="${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/docker.sock"
   129→  DOCKER="${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/docker-runtime/bin/docker"
   130→
   131→  ARCH=$(dpkg --print-architecture 2>/dev/null || echo "amd64")
   132→
   133→  # Resolve password from file if needed
   134→  if [ -z "${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD:-}" ] && [ -n "${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD_FILE:-}" ]; then
   135→    [ ! -f "$SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD_FILE" ] && die "Password file not found: $SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD_FILE"
   136→    SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD="$(cat "$SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD_FILE")"
   137→  fi
   138→}
   139→
   140→# ═══ ensure_dirs ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   141→# Create/fix data directories and ownership. Safe to run repeatedly.
   142→
   143→ensure_dirs() {
   144→  mkdir -p "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/etc
   145→  mkdir -p "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/{users,sandboxes,wetty}
   146→  mkdir -p "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/traefik/{dynamic,certs}
   147→  chown "${SANDCASTLE_USER}:${SANDCASTLE_GROUP}" "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"
   148→  # Own top-level data dirs (not -R: per-user subdirs are bind-mounted into
   149→  # Sysbox containers which use a different UID range via /etc/subuid).
   150→  chown "${SANDCASTLE_UID}:${SANDCASTLE_GID}" \
   151→    "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/users \
   152→    "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/sandboxes \
   153→    "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/wetty
   154→  chown -R "${SANDCASTLE_UID}:${SANDCASTLE_GID}" \
   155→    "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/traefik/dynamic
   156→  # Per-user dirs: own the user-level parent, then chmod 777 the bind-mount
   157→  # targets so Sysbox-mapped root can write to them.
   158→  for d in "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/users/*; do
   159→    [ -d "$d" ] && chown "${SANDCASTLE_UID}:${SANDCASTLE_GID}" "$d"
   160→  done
   161→  for d in "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/users/*/home \
   162→           "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/users/*/data \
   163→           "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/sandboxes/*/vol; do
   164→    [ -d "$d" ] && chmod 777 "$d"
   165→  done
   166→  # Fix home dir permissions inside running sandbox containers so sshd
   167→  # StrictModes is satisfied (rejects 777 home dirs).
   168→  for cid in $($DOCKER ps -q 2>/dev/null); do
   169→    user=$($DOCKER inspect --format '{{range .Config.Env}}{{println .}}{{end}}' "$cid" \
   170→      | grep '^SANDCASTLE_USER=' | cut -d= -f2 || true)
   171→    [ -n "$user" ] && $DOCKER exec "$cid" chmod 755 "/home/$user" 2>/dev/null || true
   172→  done
   173→  usermod -d "$SANDCASTLE_HOME" "$SANDCASTLE_USER" 2>/dev/null || true
   174→  ok "Data directories verified"
   175→}
   176→
   177→# ═══ Helpers ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   178→
   179→show_image_info() {
   180→  local repo="$1"
   181→  python3 -c "
   182→import urllib.request, json, sys, re
   183→from datetime import datetime, timezone
   184→repo, arch = '${repo}', '${ARCH}'
   185→try:
   186→    r = urllib.request.urlopen(f'https://ghcr.io/token?scope=repository:thieso2/{repo}:pull')
   187→    token = json.load(r)['token']
   188→    h = {'Authorization': f'Bearer {token}'}
   189→    req = urllib.request.Request(
   190→        f'https://ghcr.io/v2/thieso2/{repo}/manifests/latest',
   191→        headers={**h, 'Accept': 'application/vnd.oci.image.index.v1+json,application/vnd.oci.image.manifest.v1+json'})
   192→    m = json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(req))
   193→    if 'manifests' in m:
   194→        for p in m['manifests']:
   195→            if p.get('platform', {}).get('architecture') == arch:
   196→                req = urllib.request.Request(
   197→                    f'https://ghcr.io/v2/thieso2/{repo}/manifests/{p[\"digest\"]}',
   198→                    headers={**h, 'Accept': 'application/vnd.oci.image.manifest.v1+json'})
   199→                m = json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(req))
   200→                break
   201→    cfg = json.load(urllib.request.urlopen(
   202→        urllib.request.Request(f'https://ghcr.io/v2/thieso2/{repo}/blobs/{m[\"config\"][\"digest\"]}', headers=h)))
   203→    ts = re.sub(r'(\.\d{6})\d+', r'\1', cfg['created']).replace('Z', '+00:00')
   204→    dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   205→    secs = int((datetime.now(timezone.utc) - dt).total_seconds())
   206→    if secs < 0: secs = 0
   207→    if secs >= 86400: ago = f'{secs // 86400}d ago'
   208→    elif secs >= 3600: ago = f'{secs // 3600}h ago'
   209→    else: ago = f'{max(1, secs // 60)}m ago'
   210→    print(f'  ghcr.io/thieso2/{repo}:latest    built {dt.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC\")} ({ago})')
   211→except Exception:
   212→    print(f'  ghcr.io/thieso2/{repo}:latest    (unable to fetch build info)')
   213→" 2>/dev/null || echo "  ghcr.io/thieso2/${repo}:latest"
   214→}
   215→
   216→find_free_subnet() {
   217→  local used
   218→  used=$(
   219→    { ip route 2>/dev/null; ip addr 2>/dev/null; netstat -rn 2>/dev/null; } \
   220→      | grep -oE '(10|172|192)\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+' \
   221→      | sort -un
   222→    $DOCKER network ls -q 2>/dev/null | while read -r nid; do
   223→      $DOCKER network inspect --format '{{range .IPAM.Config}}{{.Subnet}}{{end}}' "$nid" 2>/dev/null
   224→    done | grep -oE '(10|172|192)\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+' \
   225→      | sort -un
   226→  )
   227→  for b in $(shuf -i 16-31); do
   228→    for c in $(shuf -i 1-254 | head -5); do
   229→      if ! echo "$used" | grep -q "^172\.${b}\.${c}\."; then
   230→        echo "172.${b}.${c}.0/24"
   231→        return
   232→      fi
   233→    done
   234→  done
   235→  for b in $(shuf -i 1-254 | head -20); do
   236→    for c in $(shuf -i 1-254 | head -5); do
   237→      if ! echo "$used" | grep -q "^10\.${b}\.${c}\."; then
   238→        echo "10.${b}.${c}.0/24"
   239→        return
   240→      fi
   241→    done
   242→  done
   243→  echo "172.30.99.0/24"
   244→}
   245→
   246→# ═══ write_compose ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   247→
   248→write_helper_scripts() {
   249→  cat > "${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/docker-runtime/bin/docker-logs" <<LOGS
   250→#!/bin/bash
   251→exec sudo ${DOCKER} compose -f ${SANDCASTLE_HOME}/docker-compose.yml --env-file ${SANDCASTLE_HOME}/.env logs -f "\$@"
   252→LOGS
   253→  chmod +x "${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/docker-runtime/bin/docker-logs"
   254→  wrote "${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/docker-runtime/bin/docker-logs"
   255→}
   256→
   257→write_compose() {
   258→  local DATA_MOUNT="$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data"
   259→
   260→  cat > "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/docker-compose.yml" <<COMPOSE
   261→services:
   262→  traefik:
   263→    image: traefik:v3.3
   264→    runtime: runc
   265→    restart: unless-stopped
   266→    ports:
   267→      - "${SANDCASTLE_HTTP_PORT}:80"
   268→      - "${SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT}:443"
   269→    volumes:
   270→      - ${DATA_MOUNT}/traefik/traefik.yml:/etc/traefik/traefik.yml:ro
   271→      - ${DATA_MOUNT}/traefik/dynamic:/data/dynamic:ro
   272→      - ${DATA_MOUNT}/traefik/acme.json:/data/acme.json
   273→      - ${DATA_MOUNT}/traefik/certs:/data/certs:ro
   274→    networks:
   275→      - sandcastle-web
   276→
   277→  postgres:
   278→    image: postgres:18
   279→    runtime: runc
   280→    restart: unless-stopped
   281→    volumes:
   282→      - ${SANDCASTLE_HOME}/data/postgres:/var/lib/postgresql
   283→      - ${SANDCASTLE_HOME}/etc/postgres/init-databases.sh:/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/init-databases.sh:ro
   284→    environment:
   285→      POSTGRES_USER: sandcastle
   286→      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: \${DB_PASSWORD}
   287→      POSTGRES_DB: sandcastle_production
   288→    healthcheck:
   289→      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U sandcastle -d sandcastle_production"]
   290→      interval: 5s
   291→      timeout: 5s
   292→      retries: 5
   293→    networks:
   294→      - sandcastle-web
   295→
   296→  web:
   297→    image: ${APP_IMAGE}
   298→    runtime: runc
   299→    container_name: sandcastle-web
   300→    group_add:
   301→      - "\${DOCKER_GID:-988}"
   302→    volumes:
   303→      - \${DOCKER_SOCK}:/var/run/docker.sock
   304→      - ${DATA_MOUNT}:${DATA_MOUNT}
   305→    environment:
   306→      RAILS_ENV: production
   307→      SECRET_KEY_BASE: \${SECRET_KEY_BASE}
   308→      SANDCASTLE_HOST: \${SANDCASTLE_HOST}
   309→      SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR: ${DATA_MOUNT}
   310→      SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE: \${SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE:-letsencrypt}
   311→      SANDCASTLE_SUBNET: \${SANDCASTLE_SUBNET:-172.30.99.0/24}
   312→      SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER: \${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER:-admin}
   313→      SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL: \${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL:-}
   314→      SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD: \${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD:-}
   315→      SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_SSH_KEY: \${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_SSH_KEY:-}
   316→      DB_HOST: postgres
   317→      DB_USER: sandcastle
   318→      DB_PASSWORD: \${DB_PASSWORD}
   319→      GITHUB_CLIENT_ID: \${GITHUB_CLIENT_ID:-}
   320→      GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET: \${GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET:-}
   321→      GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID: \${GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID:-}
   322→      GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET: \${GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET:-}
   323→    restart: unless-stopped
   324→    depends_on:
   325→      migrate:
   326→        condition: service_completed_successfully
   327→    networks:
   328→      - sandcastle-web
   329→
   330→  migrate:
   331→    image: ${APP_IMAGE}
   332→    runtime: runc
   333→    command: ["./bin/rails", "db:prepare"]
   334→    environment:
   335→      RAILS_ENV: production
   336→      SECRET_KEY_BASE: \${SECRET_KEY_BASE}
   337→      SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER: \${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER:-admin}
   338→      SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL: \${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL:-}
   339→      SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD: \${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD:-}
   340→      SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_SSH_KEY: \${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_SSH_KEY:-}
   341→      DB_HOST: postgres
   342→      DB_USER: sandcastle
   343→      DB_PASSWORD: \${DB_PASSWORD}
   344→    depends_on:
   345→      postgres:
   346→        condition: service_healthy
   347→    networks:
   348→      - sandcastle-web
   349→
   350→networks:
   351→  sandcastle-web:
   352→    external: true
   353→COMPOSE
   354→
   355→  wrote "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/docker-compose.yml"
   356→}
   357→
   358→# ═══ cmd_gen_env ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   359→
   360→cmd_gen_env() {
   361→  local out="${SANDCASTLE_ENV:-./sandcastle.env}"
   362→  [ -f "$out" ] && die "File already exists: $out"
   363→
   364→  local home="${SANDCASTLE_HOME:-/sandcastle}"
   365→  local app_image="${APP_IMAGE:-ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle:latest}"
   366→  local sandbox_image="${SANDBOX_IMAGE:-ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest}"
   367→  local user="${SANDCASTLE_USER:-sandcastle}"
   368→  local group="${SANDCASTLE_GROUP:-$user}"
   369→  local uid="${SANDCASTLE_UID:-220568}"
   370→  local gid="${SANDCASTLE_GID:-220568}"
   371→  local http_port="${SANDCASTLE_HTTP_PORT:-80}"
   372→  local https_port="${SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT:-443}"
   373→  local tls_mode="${SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE:-selfsigned}"
   374→
   375→  # Auto-detect host IP
   376→  local host="${SANDCASTLE_HOST:-}"
   377→  if [ -z "$host" ]; then
   378→    host=$(hostname -I 2>/dev/null | tr ' ' '\n' | grep -E '^[0-9]+\.' | head -1 || true)
   379→    host="${host:-$(curl -4fsSL --max-time 5 https://ifconfig.me 2>/dev/null || true)}"
   380→    host="${host:-0.0.0.0}"
   381→  fi
   382→
   383→  local admin_email="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL:-admin@example.com}"
   384→  local admin_user="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER:-admin}"
   385→
   386→  local dy_root="${DOCKYARD_ROOT:-$home}"
   387→  local dy_prefix="${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX:-sc_}"
   388→  local dy_bridge="${DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR:-172.42.89.1/24}"
   389→  local dy_fixed="${DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR:-172.42.89.0/24}"
   390→  local dy_pool="${DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE:-172.89.0.0/16}"
   391→  local dy_pool_size="${DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE:-24}"
   392→
   393→  cat > "$out" <<EOF
   394→# Sandcastle configuration
   395→# Edit values below, then run: sudo installer.sh install
   396→
   397→# ─── Paths & images ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   398→SANDCASTLE_HOME=${home}
   399→APP_IMAGE=${app_image}
   400→SANDBOX_IMAGE=${sandbox_image}
   401→
   402→# ─── System user (home=SANDCASTLE_HOME, shell=/bin/bash) ────────────────────
   403→SANDCASTLE_USER=${user}
   404→SANDCASTLE_GROUP=${group}
   405→SANDCASTLE_UID=${uid}
   406→SANDCASTLE_GID=${gid}
   407→
   408→# ─── Network & TLS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   409→SANDCASTLE_HOST=${host}
   410→SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE=${tls_mode}
   411→#ACME_EMAIL=admin@example.com
   412→SANDCASTLE_HTTP_PORT=${http_port}
   413→SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT=${https_port}
   414→#SANDCASTLE_SUBNET=
   415→
   416→# ─── Admin account (required for fresh install) ─────────────────────────────
   417→SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER=${admin_user}
   418→SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL=${admin_email}
   419→#SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD=changeme
   420→#REDACTED
   421→#SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_SSH_KEY=ssh-ed25519 AAAA...
   422→
   423→# ─── OAuth (optional — enables "Sign in with …" buttons) ──────────────────
   424→#GITHUB_CLIENT_ID=
   425→#GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET=
   426→#GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=
   427→#GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET=
   428→
   429→# ─── Dockyard (Docker + Sysbox) ─────────────────────────────────────────────
   430→DOCKYARD_ROOT=${dy_root}
   431→DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX=${dy_prefix}
   432→DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR=${dy_bridge}
   433→DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR=${dy_fixed}
   434→DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=${dy_pool}
   435→DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE=${dy_pool_size}
   436→EOF
   437→
   438→  ok "Generated $out — edit it, then run: sudo installer.sh install"
   439→}
   440→
   441→# ═══ cmd_destroy ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   442→
   443→cmd_destroy() {
   444→  local auto_confirm="${1:-false}"
   445→
   446→  require_root
   447→
   448→  # Load installed env for CREATED_USER/CREATED_GROUP
   449→  if [ -f "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/sandcastle.env" ]; then
   450→    # shellcheck source=/dev/null
   451→    source "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/sandcastle.env"
   452→  fi
   453→
   454→  warn "This will remove Sandcastle from $SANDCASTLE_HOME (containers, config, services)"
   455→  if [ "$auto_confirm" != "true" ]; then
   456→    read -rp "Are you sure? (yes to confirm): " CONFIRM
   457→    [ "$CONFIRM" = "yes" ] || die "Aborted"
   458→  fi
   459→
   460→  info "Tearing down Sandcastle..."
   461→  cd /
   462→
   463→  if [ -x "$DOCKER" ]; then
   464→    for container in $($DOCKER ps -a --filter "name=^sc-ts-" --format '{{.Names}}' 2>/dev/null); do
   465→      info "Removing Tailscale sidecar: $container"
   466→      $DOCKER rm -f "$container" 2>/dev/null || true
   467→    done
   468→    for container in $($DOCKER ps -a --filter "runtime=sysbox-runc" --format '{{.Names}}' 2>/dev/null); do
   469→      info "Removing container: $container"
   470→      $DOCKER rm -f "$container" 2>/dev/null || true
   471→    done
   472→    for net in $($DOCKER network ls --filter "name=^sc-ts-net-" --format '{{.Name}}' 2>/dev/null); do
   473→      info "Removing network: $net"
   474→      $DOCKER network rm "$net" 2>/dev/null || true
   475→    done
   476→    if [ -f "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/docker-compose.yml" ]; then
   477→      $DOCKER compose -f "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/docker-compose.yml" --env-file "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" down --rmi all --volumes --remove-orphans 2>/dev/null || true
   478→    fi
   479→    $DOCKER network rm sandcastle-web 2>/dev/null || true
   480→  fi
   481→
   482→  # Destroy Dockyard
   483→  DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE="$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env"
   484→  if [ -f "$DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE" ] || systemctl cat "${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker.service" &>/dev/null; then
   485→    info "Destroying Dockyard..."
   486→    if [ -f "$DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE" ] && wget -q "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/thieso2/dockyard/refs/heads/main/dockyard.sh" -O /tmp/dockyard.sh 2>/dev/null; then
   487→      echo "y" | DOCKYARD_ENV="$DOCKYARD_ENV_FILE" bash /tmp/dockyard.sh destroy >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
   488→      rm -f /tmp/dockyard.sh
   489→    else
   490→      systemctl stop "${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker" 2>/dev/null || true
   491→      systemctl disable "${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker" 2>/dev/null || true
   492→      rm -f "/etc/systemd/system/${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker.service"
   493→      systemctl daemon-reload 2>/dev/null || true
   494→      ip link delete "${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker0" 2>/dev/null || true
   495→    fi
   496→    ok "Dockyard destroyed"
   497→  fi
   498→
   499→  # Remove user/group if we created them
   500→  if [ "${CREATED_USER:-}" = "true" ] && id "$SANDCASTLE_USER" &>/dev/null; then
   501→    userdel "$SANDCASTLE_USER" 2>/dev/null || true
   502→    ok "Removed user '${SANDCASTLE_USER}'"
   503→  fi
   504→  if [ "${CREATED_GROUP:-}" = "true" ] && getent group "$SANDCASTLE_GROUP" &>/dev/null; then
   505→    groupdel "$SANDCASTLE_GROUP" 2>/dev/null || true
   506→    ok "Removed group '${SANDCASTLE_GROUP}'"
   507→  fi
   508→
   509→  # Remove NAT service
   510→  if [ -f /etc/systemd/system/sandcastle-nat.service ]; then
   511→    systemctl disable --now sandcastle-nat 2>/dev/null || true
   512→    rm -f /etc/systemd/system/sandcastle-nat.service
   513→    systemctl daemon-reload 2>/dev/null || true
   514→  fi
   515→
   516→  # Remove Sandcastle files — keep user data (data/users, data/sandboxes)
   517→  rm -f "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
   518→  rm -f "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/docker-compose.yml"
   519→  rm -rf "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc"
   520→  rm -rf "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/traefik"
   521→  rmdir "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data" 2>/dev/null || true
   522→  rmdir "$SANDCASTLE_HOME" 2>/dev/null || true
   523→  rm -rf "/run/${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}docker"
   524→
   525→  if [ -d "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/users" ] || [ -d "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/sandboxes" ]; then
   526→    warn "User data preserved in $SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/ — remove manually if no longer needed"
   527→  fi
   528→
   529→  ok "Sandcastle destroyed"
   530→}
   531→
   532→# ═══ cmd_install ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   533→
   534→cmd_install() {
   535→  require_root
   536→
   537→  if ! grep -qi ubuntu /etc/os-release 2>/dev/null; then
   538→    warn "This script is tested on Ubuntu 24.04. Other distros may work but are unsupported."
   539→  fi
   540→  if [ "$ARCH" != "amd64" ] && [ "$ARCH" != "arm64" ]; then
   541→    die "Sandcastle requires amd64 or arm64 architecture (got: $ARCH)"
   542→  fi
   543→
   544→  echo ""
   545→  echo -e "${BLUE}═══ Sandcastle Installer ═══${NC}"
   546→  echo ""
   547→  info "Available images (${ARCH}):"
   548→  show_image_info "sandcastle"
   549→  show_image_info "sandcastle-sandbox"
   550→  echo ""
   551→
   552→  # ── Install Dockyard ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   553→
   554→  if [ -S "$DOCKER_SOCK" ]; then
   555→    ok "Dockyard already installed"
   556→  else
   557→    info "Installing Dockyard (Docker + Sysbox)..."
   558→    wget -q "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/thieso2/dockyard/refs/heads/main/dockyard.sh" -O /tmp/dockyard.sh
   559→
   560→    mkdir -p "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc"
   561→    cat > "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env" <<DYEOF
   562→DOCKYARD_ROOT=${DOCKYARD_ROOT}
   563→DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX=${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}
   564→DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR=${DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR}
   565→DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR=${DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR}
   566→DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE=${DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE}
   567→DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE=${DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE}
   568→DYEOF
   569→    wrote "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env"
   570→
   571→    DOCKYARD_ENV="$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/dockyard.env" bash /tmp/dockyard.sh create
   572→    rm -f /tmp/dockyard.sh
   573→
   574→    for i in $(seq 1 30); do
   575→      [ -S "$DOCKER_SOCK" ] && break
   576→      sleep 1
   577→    done
   578→    [ -S "$DOCKER_SOCK" ] || die "Dockyard socket not found at $DOCKER_SOCK after 30s"
   579→    ok "Dockyard installed"
   580→  fi
   581→
   582→  # ── Configure UFW ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   583→
   584→  if command -v ufw &>/dev/null; then
   585→    info "Configuring firewall..."
   586→    ufw --force reset >/dev/null 2>&1
   587→    ufw default deny incoming >/dev/null 2>&1
   588→    ufw default allow outgoing >/dev/null 2>&1
   589→    ufw allow 22/tcp >/dev/null 2>&1
   590→    ufw allow "${SANDCASTLE_HTTP_PORT}/tcp" >/dev/null 2>&1
   591→    ufw allow "${SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT}/tcp" >/dev/null 2>&1
   592→    ufw allow 2201:2299/tcp >/dev/null 2>&1
   593→    ufw --force enable >/dev/null 2>&1
   594→    ok "Firewall configured (22, ${SANDCASTLE_HTTP_PORT}, ${SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT}, 2201-2299)"
   595→  else
   596→    warn "UFW not found — skipping firewall setup"
   597→  fi
   598→
   599→  # ── Create system user/group ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   600→
   601→  CREATED_GROUP=false
   602→  CREATED_USER=false
   603→
   604→  if getent group "$SANDCASTLE_GROUP" &>/dev/null; then
   605→    ok "Group '${SANDCASTLE_GROUP}' already exists"
   606→  else
   607→    groupadd --system --gid "$SANDCASTLE_GID" "$SANDCASTLE_GROUP"
   608→    CREATED_GROUP=true
   609→    ok "Created group '${SANDCASTLE_GROUP}' (GID ${SANDCASTLE_GID})"
   610→  fi
   611→
   612→  if id "$SANDCASTLE_USER" &>/dev/null; then
   613→    ok "User '${SANDCASTLE_USER}' already exists"
   614→  else
   615→    useradd --system --uid "$SANDCASTLE_UID" --gid "$SANDCASTLE_GID" \
   616→      --home-dir "$SANDCASTLE_HOME" --shell /bin/bash "$SANDCASTLE_USER"
   617→    CREATED_USER=true
   618→    ok "Created user '${SANDCASTLE_USER}' (UID ${SANDCASTLE_UID})"
   619→  fi
   620→
   621→  # ── Create directories ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   622→
   623→  ensure_dirs
   624→
   625→  # ── Detect fresh install vs upgrade ─────────────────────────────────────
   626→
   627→  FRESH_INSTALL=false
   628→  [ ! -f "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" ] && FRESH_INSTALL=true
   629→
   630→  # ── Fresh install: validate & generate .env ─────────────────────────────
   631→
   632→  if [ "$FRESH_INSTALL" = true ]; then
   633→    [ -z "${SANDCASTLE_HOST:-}" ] && die "SANDCASTLE_HOST is required (set in sandcastle.env)"
   634→    [ -z "${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL:-}" ] && die "SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL is required (set in sandcastle.env)"
   635→    [ -z "${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD:-}" ] && die "SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD is required (set in sandcastle.env or use SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD_FILE)"
   636→    [ ${#SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD} -lt 6 ] && die "SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD must be at least 6 characters"
   637→
   638→    if [ -z "${SANDCASTLE_SUBNET:-}" ]; then
   639→      SANDCASTLE_SUBNET=$(find_free_subnet)
   640→      info "Auto-detected subnet: $SANDCASTLE_SUBNET"
   641→    fi
   642→
   643→    SECRET_KEY_BASE=$(openssl rand -hex 64)
   644→    DB_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
   645→    DOCKER_GID=$(stat -c '%g' "$DOCKER_SOCK" 2>/dev/null || echo "988")
   646→
   647→    cat > "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" <<EOF
   648→# Sandcastle runtime — generated $(date -Iseconds)
   649→SANDCASTLE_HOME="${SANDCASTLE_HOME}"
   650→SANDCASTLE_HOST="${SANDCASTLE_HOST}"
   651→SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE="${SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE}"
   652→SECRET_KEY_BASE="${SECRET_KEY_BASE}"
   653→DB_PASSWORD="${DB_PASSWORD}"
   654→SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER}"
   655→SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL}"
   656→SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD}"
   657→SANDCASTLE_SUBNET="${SANDCASTLE_SUBNET}"
   658→SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_SSH_KEY="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_SSH_KEY:-}"
   659→DOCKER_GID="${DOCKER_GID}"
   660→DOCKER_SOCK="${DOCKER_SOCK}"
   661→ACME_EMAIL="${ACME_EMAIL:-}"
   662→GITHUB_CLIENT_ID="${GITHUB_CLIENT_ID:-}"
   663→GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET="${GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET:-}"
   664→GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID="${GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID:-}"
   665→GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET="${GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET:-}"
   666→EOF
   667→    chmod 600 "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
   668→    wrote "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
   669→  else
   670→    info "Existing install — loading $SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
   671→  fi
   672→
   673→  # shellcheck source=/dev/null
   674→  source "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
   675→
   676→  # Backfill vars that may be missing in older .env files
   677→  if [ -z "${SANDCASTLE_SUBNET:-}" ]; then
   678→    SANDCASTLE_SUBNET=$(find_free_subnet)
   679→    echo "SANDCASTLE_SUBNET=$SANDCASTLE_SUBNET" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
   680→  fi
   681→  if [ -z "${DOCKER_SOCK:-}" ]; then
   682→    DOCKER_SOCK="${DOCKYARD_ROOT}/docker.sock"
   683→    echo "DOCKER_SOCK=$DOCKER_SOCK" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
   684→  fi
   685→  # Backfill OAuth vars from sandcastle.env into .env
   686→  grep -q '^GITHUB_CLIENT_ID=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \
   687→    echo "GITHUB_CLIENT_ID=${GITHUB_CLIENT_ID:-}" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
   688→  grep -q '^GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \
   689→    echo "GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET=${GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET:-}" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
   690→  grep -q '^GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \
   691→    echo "GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=${GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID:-}" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
   692→  grep -q '^GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \
   693→    echo "GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET=${GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET:-}" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
   694→
   695→  # ── Write installed sandcastle.env ────────────────────────────────────────
   696→
   697→  cat > "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/sandcastle.env" <<EOF
   698→# Sandcastle installed configuration — generated $(date -Iseconds)
   699→SANDCASTLE_HOME="${SANDCASTLE_HOME}"
   700→APP_IMAGE="${APP_IMAGE}"
   701→SANDBOX_IMAGE="${SANDBOX_IMAGE}"
   702→SANDCASTLE_USER="${SANDCASTLE_USER}"
   703→SANDCASTLE_GROUP="${SANDCASTLE_GROUP}"
   704→SANDCASTLE_UID="${SANDCASTLE_UID}"
   705→SANDCASTLE_GID="${SANDCASTLE_GID}"
   706→SANDCASTLE_HOST="${SANDCASTLE_HOST}"
   707→SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE="${SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE}"
   708→SANDCASTLE_HTTP_PORT="${SANDCASTLE_HTTP_PORT}"
   709→SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT="${SANDCASTLE_HTTPS_PORT}"
   710→SANDCASTLE_SUBNET="${SANDCASTLE_SUBNET}"
   711→SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER}"
   712→SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL:-}"
   713→SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_SSH_KEY="${SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_SSH_KEY:-}"
   714→ACME_EMAIL="${ACME_EMAIL:-}"
   715→DOCKYARD_ROOT="${DOCKYARD_ROOT}"
   716→DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX="${DOCKYARD_DOCKER_PREFIX}"
   717→DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR="${DOCKYARD_BRIDGE_CIDR}"
   718→DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR="${DOCKYARD_FIXED_CIDR}"
   719→DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE="${DOCKYARD_POOL_BASE}"
   720→DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE="${DOCKYARD_POOL_SIZE}"
   721→DOCKER_SOCK="${DOCKER_SOCK}"
   722→DOCKER_GID="${DOCKER_GID:-988}"
   723→GITHUB_CLIENT_ID="${GITHUB_CLIENT_ID:-}"
   724→GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET="${GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET:-}"
   725→GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID="${GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID:-}"
   726→GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET="${GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET:-}"
   727→CREATED_USER="${CREATED_USER}"
   728→CREATED_GROUP="${CREATED_GROUP}"
   729→EOF
   730→  chmod 600 "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/sandcastle.env"
   731→  wrote "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/etc/sandcastle.env"
   732→
   733→  # ── Traefik config ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   734→
   735→  TRAEFIK_DIR="$SANDCASTLE_HOME/data/traefik"
   736→
   737→  if [ "$SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE" = "selfsigned" ]; then
   738→    if [ ! -f "$TRAEFIK_DIR/certs/cert.pem" ]; then
   739→      info "Generating self-signed certificate..."
   740→      openssl req -x509 -newkey rsa:4096 -sha256 -days 3650 -nodes \
   741→        -keyout "$TRAEFIK_DIR/certs/key.pem" -out "$TRAEFIK_DIR/certs/cert.pem" \
   742→        -subj "/CN=$SANDCASTLE_HOST" \
   743→        -addext "subjectAltName=IP:$SANDCASTLE_HOST" 2>/dev/null
   744→      ok "Self-signed certificate generated"
   745→    fi
   746→
   747→    cat > "$TRAEFIK_DIR/traefik.yml" <<'TEOF'
   748→entryPoints:
   749→  web:
   750→    address: ":80"
   751→    http:
   752→      redirections:
   753→        entryPoint:
   754→          to: websecure
   755→          scheme: https
   756→  websecure:
   757→    address: ":443"
   758→
   759→providers:
   760→  file:
   761→    directory: /data/dynamic
   762→    watch: true

   854→  # ── Docker network ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

   863→  # ── NAT & forwarding for sandcastle networks ─────────────────────────────
   864→  # Dockyard runs with --iptables=false, so user-defined networks (sandcastle-web,
   865→  # per-user Tailscale bridges) have no MASQUERADE or FORWARD rules.
   866→  # Cover all of them with a single /16 derived from SANDCASTLE_SUBNET.
   867→
   868→  SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR=$(echo "$SANDCASTLE_SUBNET" | awk -F'[./]' '{print $1"."$2".0.0/16"}')
   869→
   870→  iptables -t nat -C POSTROUTING -s "$SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR" -j MASQUERADE 2>/dev/null || \
   871→    iptables -t nat -A POSTROUTING -s "$SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR" -j MASQUERADE
   872→  iptables -C FORWARD -s "$SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR" -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || \
   873→    iptables -I FORWARD -s "$SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR" -j ACCEPT
   874→  iptables -C FORWARD -d "$SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR" -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || \
   875→    iptables -I FORWARD -d "$SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR" -j ACCEPT

   878→  # Persist across reboots via systemd

   881→Description=Sandcastle NAT and forwarding rules

   883→
   884→[Service]
   885→Type=oneshot

   887→ExecStart=/bin/sh -c 'iptables -C FORWARD -s ${SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR} -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || iptables -I FORWARD -s ${SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR} -j ACCEPT'
   888→ExecStart=/bin/sh -c 'iptables -C FORWARD -d ${SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR} -j ACCEPT 2>/dev/null || iptables -I FORWARD -d ${SANDCASTLE_NAT_CIDR} -j ACCEPT'
   889→RemainAfterExit=yes
   890→
   891→[Install]
   892→WantedBy=multi-user.target
   893→NATEOF

   933→

   935→
   936→  if [ "$FRESH_INSTALL" = true ]; then
   937→    info "Waiting for app to be ready..."
   938→    for i in $(seq 1 30); do

   952→  fi
   953→

   990→
   991→# ═══ cmd_update ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   992→
   993→cmd_update() {
   994→  require_root
   995→
   996→  [ ! -f "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" ] && die "No existing install found at $SANDCASTLE_HOME — run 'install' first"
   997→
   998→  # shellcheck source=/dev/null
   999→  source "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
  1000→
  1001→  # Backfill vars that may be missing in older .env files
  1002→  grep -q '^GITHUB_CLIENT_ID=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \
  1003→    echo "GITHUB_CLIENT_ID=${GITHUB_CLIENT_ID:-}" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
  1004→  grep -q '^GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \
  1005→    echo "GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET=${GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET:-}" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
  1006→  grep -q '^GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \

  1008→  grep -q '^GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET=' "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env" 2>/dev/null || \
  1009→    echo "GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET=${GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET:-}" >> "$SANDCASTLE_HOME/.env"
  1010→
  1011→  echo ""
  1012→  echo -e "${BLUE}═══ Sandcastle Update ═══${NC}"
  1013→  echo ""
  1014→  info "Available images (${ARCH}):"
  1015→  show_image_info "sandcastle"
  1016→  show_image_info "sandcastle-sandbox"
  1017→  echo ""
  1018→
  1019→  ensure_dirs

  1021→  info "Pulling images..."
  1022→  $DOCKER pull "$APP_IMAGE" &
  1023→  $DOCKER pull "$SANDBOX_IMAGE" &
  1024→  wait
  1025→  ok "Images pulled"
  1026→
  1027→  write_compose
  1028→  write_helper_scripts
  1029→
  1030→  info "Restarting services..."
  1031→  cd "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"
  1032→  $DOCKER compose --env-file .env up -d
  1033→  ok "Services restarted"
  1034→
  1035→  echo ""
  1036→  echo -e "${GREEN}  Sandcastle updated!${NC}"
  1037→
  1038→  print_written_files
  1039→  echo ""
  1040→}
  1041→
  1042→# ═══ Dispatch ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  1043→
  1044→case "$COMMAND" in
  1045→  gen-env)    cmd_gen_env ;;
  1046→  install)    load_env; derive_vars; cmd_install ;;
  1047→  update)     load_env; derive_vars; cmd_update ;;
  1048→  reset)      load_env; derive_vars; cmd_destroy true; cmd_install ;;
  1049→  uninstall)  load_env; derive_vars; cmd_destroy ;;
  1050→esac
```

> AGENT

I see the issue. The `SANDCASTLE_USER` (along with `SANDCASTLE_GROUP`, `SANDCASTLE_UID`, `SANDCASTLE_GID`) variables are written to `etc/sandcastle.env` but not to the runtime `.env` file used by Docker Compose. While they're not currently referenced in the Docker Compose file, they should be included for consistency and potential future use.

> AGENT

Fixed! The `db/seeds.rb` file was ignoring the `SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER` environment variable and deriving the username from the email address instead. Now it will:

1. Use `SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_USER` if provided (which the installer sets)
2. Fall back to deriving from the email if not provided (for backwards compatibility)

The admin user will now be created with the configured username from `sandcastle.env` instead of being auto-generated from the email.

> AGENT

Added the `deploy:local:reset` task to `mise.toml`. It will:

1. Stop all containers from the local docker-compose deployment
2. Remove volumes (`--volumes`)
3. Remove orphan containers (`--remove-orphans`)
4. Clean up networks

Usage:
```bash
mise run deploy:local:reset
```

After resetting, you can rebuild and start fresh with:
```bash
mise run deploy:local
```

> AGENT

Based on `db/seeds.rb`, the fallback defaults are:

- **Email**: `admin@sandcastle.rocks`
- **Password**: `sandcastle`
- **Username**: `admin` (derived from email)

**However**, the installer requires you to set `SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_EMAIL` and `SANDCASTLE_ADMIN_PASSWORD` in `sandcastle.env` - it won't proceed without them. So for a proper installation, there are no "defaults" - you must provide credentials.

These fallback values only apply if you run `rails db:seed` directly without setting the environment variables (e.g., during local development).

The user is also created with `must_change_password: true`, so they'll be prompted to change their password on first login.

```
     1→class TerminalManager
     2→  DATA_DIR = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_DATA_DIR", "/data")
     3→  WETTY_IMAGE = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_WETTY_IMAGE", "wettyoss/wetty:latest")
     4→  NETWORK_NAME = "sandcastle-web"
     5→  DYNAMIC_DIR = File.join(DATA_DIR, "traefik", "dynamic")
     6→
     7→  class Error < StandardError; end
     8→
     9→  # Opens a web terminal for the given sandbox.
    10→  # Returns the URL path to the WeTTY session.
    11→  def open(sandbox:)
    12→    raise Error, "Sandbox is not running" unless sandbox.status == "running"
    13→    raise Error, "Sandbox has no container" if sandbox.container_id.blank?
    14→
    15→    user = sandbox.user
    16→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
    17→
    18→    # Idempotent: if WeTTY container already running, return URL
    19→    if container_running?(container_name)
    20→      return wetty_url(sandbox)
    21→    end
    22→
    23→    pull_image
    24→    ensure_network
    25→    connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
    26→
    27→    # Write Traefik config early so it has time to detect the new route
    28→    # while we set up keypairs and start the WeTTY container.
    29→    write_traefik_config(sandbox)
    30→
    31→    key_dir = generate_keypair(sandbox)
    32→    inject_pubkey(sandbox, key_dir)
    33→    create_wetty_container(sandbox: sandbox, user: user, key_dir: key_dir)
    34→
    35→    wetty_url(sandbox)
    36→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    37→    raise Error, "Failed to open terminal: #{e.message}"
    38→  rescue SystemCallError => e
    39→    raise Error, "Failed to open terminal: #{e.message}"
    40→  end
    41→
    42→  # Closes the web terminal for the given sandbox.
    43→  def close(sandbox:)
    44→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
    45→
    46→    # Stop and remove WeTTY container
    47→    begin
    48→      container = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
    49→      container.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
    50→      container.delete(force: true)
    51→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
    52→      # Already gone
    53→    end
    54→
    55→    # Delete Traefik config
    56→    delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
    57→
    58→    # Clean up key directory
    59→    key_dir = key_dir_path(sandbox)
    60→    FileUtils.rm_rf(key_dir)
    61→
    62→    # Best-effort: remove pubkey from sandbox authorized_keys
    63→    remove_pubkey(sandbox)
    64→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
    65→    Rails.logger.error("TerminalManager: close failed for #{sandbox.full_name}: #{e.message}")
    66→  end
    67→
    68→  # Returns true if the WeTTY container is running for this sandbox.
    69→  def active?(sandbox:)
    70→    container_running?(wetty_container_name(sandbox))
    71→  end
    72→
    73→  # Removes orphaned WeTTY containers whose sandbox no longer exists or is not running.
    74→  def cleanup_orphaned
    75→    Docker::Container.all(all: true).each do |container|
    76→      name = container.info.dig("Names")&.first&.delete_prefix("/")
    77→      next unless name&.start_with?("sc-wetty-")
    78→
    79→      labels = container.info["Labels"] || {}
    80→      sandbox_id = labels["sandcastle.sandbox_id"]&.to_i
    81→
    82→      sandbox = sandbox_id ? Sandbox.find_by(id: sandbox_id) : nil
    83→      should_remove = sandbox.nil? || sandbox.status != "running"
    84→
    85→      if should_remove
    86→        container.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
    87→        container.delete(force: true)
    88→        Rails.logger.info("TerminalManager: removed orphaned WeTTY container #{name}")
    89→
    90→        # Clean up Traefik config and keys if we have a sandbox_id
    91→        if sandbox_id
    92→          config_path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{sandbox_id}.yml")
    93→          File.delete(config_path) if File.exist?(config_path)
    94→        end
    95→
    96→        # Extract full_name from container name and validate format
    97→        full_name = name.delete_prefix("sc-wetty-")
    98→        unless full_name.match?(/\A[a-z][a-z0-9_-]+-[a-z][a-z0-9_-]*\z/)
    99→          Rails.logger.warn("TerminalManager: skipping suspicious container name: #{name}")
   100→          next
   101→        end
   102→
   103→        key_dir = File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty", full_name)
   104→        expected_parent = File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty")
   105→        unless File.expand_path(key_dir).start_with?("#{File.expand_path(expected_parent)}/")
   106→          Rails.logger.warn("TerminalManager: path traversal attempt detected: #{key_dir}")
   107→          next
   108→        end
   109→
   110→        FileUtils.rm_rf(key_dir) if Dir.exist?(key_dir)
   111→      end
   112→    end
   113→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError => e
   114→    Rails.logger.error("TerminalManager: orphan cleanup failed: #{e.message}")
   115→  end
   116→
   117→  private
   118→
   119→  def wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   120→    "sc-wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}"
   121→  end
   122→
   123→  def wetty_url(sandbox)
   124→    "/terminal/#{sandbox.id}/wetty"
   125→  end
   126→
   127→  def key_dir_path(sandbox)
   128→    File.join(DATA_DIR, "wetty", sandbox.full_name)
   129→  end
   130→
   131→  def container_running?(name)
   132→    container = Docker::Container.get(name)
   133→    container.json.dig("State", "Running") == true
   134→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   135→    false
   136→  end
   137→
   138→  def pull_image
   139→    Docker::Image.get(WETTY_IMAGE)
   140→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   141→    Docker::Image.create("fromImage" => WETTY_IMAGE)
   142→  rescue Docker::Error::DockerError
   143→    raise Error, "Failed to pull #{WETTY_IMAGE} — check network connectivity"
   144→  end
   145→
   146→  def ensure_network
   147→    Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   148→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   149→    Docker::Network.create(NETWORK_NAME, "Driver" => "bridge")
   150→  end
   151→
   152→  def connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)
   153→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   154→
   155→    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)
   156→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   157→
   158→    networks = container.json.dig("NetworkSettings", "Networks") || {}
   159→    return if networks.key?(NETWORK_NAME)
   160→
   161→    network.connect(sandbox.container_id)
   162→  end
   163→
   164→  def generate_keypair(sandbox)
   165→    key_dir = key_dir_path(sandbox)
   166→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(key_dir, mode: 0o700)
   167→
   168→    key_path = File.join(key_dir, "key")
   169→    # Remove old keys if they exist
   170→    FileUtils.rm_f(key_path)
   171→    FileUtils.rm_f("#{key_path}.pub")
   172→
   173→    system("ssh-keygen", "-t", "ed25519", "-f", key_path, "-N", "", "-q", "-C", "wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}",
   174→      exception: true)
   175→
   176→    # Ensure private key is only readable by owner
   177→    File.chmod(0o600, key_path)
   178→
   179→    key_dir
   180→  end
   181→
   182→  def inject_pubkey(sandbox, key_dir)
   183→    pubkey = File.read(File.join(key_dir, "key.pub")).strip
   184→    username = sandbox.user.name
   185→
   186→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   187→
   188→    # Write pubkey via base64 to avoid any shell-injection risk.
   189→    # The stdin: approach hangs because docker-api never sends EOF to cat.
   190→    encoded = Base64.strict_encode64("#{pubkey}\n")
   191→    container.exec([ "mkdir", "-p", "/home/#{username}/.ssh" ])
   192→    container.exec([ "sh", "-c", "echo #{encoded} | base64 -d >> /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys" ])
   193→    container.exec([ "chown", "-R", "#{username}:#{username}", "/home/#{username}/.ssh" ])
   194→    container.exec([ "chmod", "600", "/home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys" ])
   195→  end
   196→
   197→  def create_wetty_container(sandbox:, user:, key_dir:)
   198→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   199→
   200→    # Remove any existing container with this name
   201→    begin
   202→      old = Docker::Container.get(container_name)
   203→      old.stop(t: 3) rescue nil
   204→      old.delete(force: true)
   205→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
   206→      # No existing container
   207→    end
   208→
   209→    ssh_command = [
   210→      "ssh", "-p", "22",
   211→      "-o", "StrictHostKeyChecking=no",
   212→      "-o", "UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null",
   213→      "-o", "LogLevel=ERROR",
   214→      "-i", "/etc/wetty/key",
   215→      "#{user.name}@#{sandbox.full_name}",
   216→      "-t", "tmux new-session -A -s main"
   217→    ].join(" ")
   218→
   219→    container = Docker::Container.create(
   220→      "name" => container_name,
   221→      "Image" => WETTY_IMAGE,
   222→      "Env" => [
   223→        "COMMAND=#{ssh_command}",
   224→        "BASE=/terminal/#{sandbox.id}/wetty"
   225→      ],
   226→      "HostConfig" => {
   227→        "NetworkMode" => NETWORK_NAME,
   228→        "RestartPolicy" => { "Name" => "no" },
   229→        "Memory" => 128 * 1024 * 1024, # 128MB
   230→        "NanoCpus" => 500_000_000 # 0.5 CPU
   231→      },
   232→      "Labels" => {
   233→        "sandcastle.sandbox_id" => sandbox.id.to_s,
   234→        "sandcastle.role" => "wetty"
   235→      }
   236→    )
   237→
   238→    container.start
   239→    copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)
   240→    container
   241→  end
   242→
   243→  # Copy the SSH private key into the WeTTY container via exec + base64.
   244→  # This avoids bind-mount path issues when Rails runs inside a container
   245→  # with a Docker volume for /data (the host path doesn't match).
   246→  def copy_key_to_container(container, key_dir)
   247→    key_content = File.read(File.join(key_dir, "key"))
   248→    encoded = Base64.strict_encode64(key_content)
   249→
   250→    container.exec([ "mkdir", "-p", "/etc/wetty" ])
   251→    container.exec([ "sh", "-c", "echo #{encoded} | base64 -d > /etc/wetty/key" ])
   252→    container.exec([ "chmod", "600", "/etc/wetty/key" ])
   253→  end
   254→
   255→  def write_traefik_config(sandbox)
   256→    FileUtils.mkdir_p(DYNAMIC_DIR)
   257→
   258→    host = ENV.fetch("SANDCASTLE_HOST", "localhost")
   259→    id = sandbox.id
   260→    container_name = wetty_container_name(sandbox)
   261→
   262→    rule = if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   263→      "HostRegexp(`.+`) && PathPrefix(`/terminal/#{id}/wetty`)"
   264→    else
   265→      "Host(`#{host}`) && PathPrefix(`/terminal/#{id}/wetty`)"
   266→    end
   267→
   268→    config = {
   269→      "http" => {
   270→        "routers" => {
   271→          "terminal-#{id}" => {
   272→            "rule" => rule,
   273→            "service" => "terminal-#{id}",
   274→            "entryPoints" => [ "websecure" ],
   275→            "tls" => tls_config,
   276→            "middlewares" => [ "terminal-auth-#{id}" ],
   277→            "priority" => 100
   278→          }
   279→        },
   280→        "middlewares" => {
   281→          "terminal-auth-#{id}" => {
   282→            "forwardAuth" => {
   283→              "address" => "http://sandcastle-web:80/terminal/auth",
   284→              "trustForwardHeader" => true
   285→            }
   286→          }
   287→        },
   288→        "services" => {
   289→          "terminal-#{id}" => {
   290→            "loadBalancer" => {
   291→              "servers" => [ { "url" => "http://#{container_name}:3000" } ]
   292→            }
   293→          }
   294→        }
   295→      }
   296→    }
   297→
   298→    File.write(File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{id}.yml"), config.to_yaml)
   299→  end
   300→
   301→  def tls_config
   302→    if ENV["SANDCASTLE_TLS_MODE"] == "selfsigned"
   303→      {}
   304→    else
   305→      { "certResolver" => "letsencrypt" }
   306→    end
   307→  end
   308→
   309→  def delete_traefik_config(sandbox)
   310→    path = File.join(DYNAMIC_DIR, "terminal-#{sandbox.id}.yml")
   311→    File.delete(path) if File.exist?(path)
   312→  end
   313→
   314→  def remove_pubkey(sandbox)
   315→    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?
   316→
   317→    begin
   318→      container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
   319→      username = sandbox.user.name
   320→      # Match end-of-line to avoid substring collisions
   321→      # (e.g. "wetty-user-foo" must not also remove "wetty-user-foobar").
   322→      # Sandbox names are [a-z0-9_-] only, so safe for regex.
   323→      marker = "wetty-#{sandbox.full_name}$"
   324→      container.exec([
   325→        "sh", "-c",
   326→        "grep -v '#{marker}' /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys > /tmp/ak_clean && " \
   327→        "mv /tmp/ak_clean /home/#{username}/.ssh/authorized_keys || true"
   328→      ])
   329→    rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError, Docker::Error::DockerError
   330→      # Sandbox container gone or exec failed — best-effort
   331→    end
   332→  end
   333→end
   334→
```

```
     1→class TerminalController < ApplicationController
     2→  allow_unauthenticated_access only: :auth
     3→  skip_before_action :require_password_change, only: :auth
     4→
     5→  def open
     6→    sandbox = find_sandbox
     7→
     8→    TerminalManager.new.open(sandbox: sandbox)
     9→    redirect_to terminal_wait_sandbox_path(sandbox), status: :see_other
    10→  rescue TerminalManager::Error => e
    11→    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message
    12→  end
    13→
    14→  def wait
    15→    @sandbox = find_sandbox
    16→    @terminal_url = terminal_redirect_url("/terminal/#{@sandbox.id}/wetty")
    17→  end
    18→
    19→  def status
    20→    sandbox = find_sandbox
    21→    ready = TerminalManager.new.active?(sandbox: sandbox)
    22→    render json: { status: ready ? "ready" : "waiting" }
    23→  end
    24→
    25→  def close
    26→    sandbox = find_sandbox
    27→
    28→    TerminalManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
    29→    redirect_to root_path, notice: "Terminal closed"
    30→  rescue TerminalManager::Error => e
    31→    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message
    32→  end
    33→
    34→  private
    35→
    36→  def find_sandbox
    37→    if Current.user.admin?
    38→      Sandbox.active.find(params[:id])
    39→    else
    40→      Current.user.sandboxes.active.find(params[:id])
    41→    end
    42→  end
    43→
    44→  # Build the full terminal URL. In production, Traefik is the entry point
    45→  # so a relative path works. In local dev (selfsigned TLS), Rails may be
    46→  # accessed directly on a different port, so we need an absolute URL.
    47→  def terminal_redirect_url(path)
    48→    base = ENV["SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL"]
    49→    base ? "#{base}#{path}" : path
    50→  end
    51→
    52→  public
    53→
    54→  # Called by Traefik forwardAuth. Returns 200 to allow, or redirects to
    55→  # login (which Traefik passes through to the browser).
    56→  def auth
    57→    forwarded_uri = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Uri"] || ""
    58→    match = forwarded_uri.match(%r{/terminal/(\d+)/wetty})
    59→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless match
    60→
    61→    session_record = find_session_by_cookie
    62→    unless session_record
    63→      # Build the original terminal URL from Traefik's forwarded headers
    64→      # so the user returns here after logging in.
    65→      proto = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Proto"] || "https"
    66→      host  = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Host"] || request.host_with_port
    67→      original_url = "#{proto}://#{host}#{forwarded_uri}"
    68→      session[:return_to_after_authenticating] = original_url
    69→      redirect_to new_session_url, allow_other_host: true
    70→      return
    71→    end
    72→
    73→    user = session_record.user
    74→    sandbox = Sandbox.active.find_by(id: match[1].to_i)
    75→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless sandbox && (sandbox.user_id == user.id || user.admin?)
    76→
    77→    head :ok
    78→  end
    79→end
```

```
     1→class ContainerSyncJob < ApplicationJob
     2→  queue_as :default
     3→
     4→  def perform
     5→    Sandbox.active.where.not(container_id: nil).find_each do |sandbox|
     6→      sync_sandbox(sandbox)
     7→    end
     8→
     9→    User.where(tailscale_state: [ "enabled", "pending" ]).find_each do |user|
    10→      sync_tailscale_sidecar(user)
    11→    end
    12→
    13→    begin
    14→      RouteManager.new.sync_all_configs
    15→    rescue => e
    16→      Rails.logger.error("ContainerSyncJob: route sync failed: #{e.message}")
    17→    end
    18→
    19→    begin
    20→      TerminalManager.new.cleanup_orphaned
    21→    rescue => e
    22→      Rails.logger.error("ContainerSyncJob: terminal cleanup failed: #{e.message}")
    23→    end
    24→  end
    25→
    26→  private
    27→
    28→  def sync_sandbox(sandbox)
    29→    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)
    30→    state = container.json["State"] || {}
    31→    actual_status = if state["Restarting"]
    32→      "stopped"
    33→    elsif state["Running"]
    34→      "running"
    35→    else
    36→      "stopped"
    37→    end
    38→
    39→    if sandbox.status != actual_status
    40→      if actual_status == "stopped"
    41→        begin
    42→          TerminalManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
    43→        rescue TerminalManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
    44→          # best-effort
    45→        end
    46→      end
    47→      sandbox.update!(status: actual_status)
    48→      Rails.logger.info("ContainerSyncJob: #{sandbox.full_name} status corrected to #{actual_status}")
    49→    end
    50→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
    51→    begin
    52→      TerminalManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
    53→    rescue TerminalManager::Error, Docker::Error::DockerError
    54→      # best-effort
    55→    end
    56→    sandbox.update!(status: "destroyed", container_id: nil)
    57→    Rails.logger.warn("ContainerSyncJob: #{sandbox.full_name} container gone, marked destroyed")
    58→  end
    59→
    60→  def sync_tailscale_sidecar(user)
    61→    return if user.tailscale_container_id.blank?
    62→
    63→    Docker::Container.get(user.tailscale_container_id)
    64→  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError
    65→    user.update!(tailscale_state: "disabled", tailscale_container_id: nil, tailscale_network: nil)
    66→    user.sandboxes.active.where(tailscale: true).update_all(tailscale: false)
    67→    Rails.logger.warn("ContainerSyncJob: Tailscale sidecar for #{user.name} gone, marked disabled")
    68→  end
    69→end
    70→
```

> AGENT

I see the issue. The `TerminalManager#connect_sandbox_to_network` method tries to connect a container that no longer exists. The `ContainerSyncJob` would eventually fix this (runs every 5 minutes), but the terminal open fails immediately.

The fix is to handle the missing container gracefully in `connect_sandbox_to_network`:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_015yZagmHmyiTw5yYRQJNAbj
```json

  "new_string": "  def connect_sandbox_to_network(sandbox)\n    return unless sandbox.container_id.present?\n\n    network = Docker::Network.get(NETWORK_NAME)\n    container = Docker::Container.get(sandbox.container_id)\n\n    networks = container.json.dig(\"NetworkSettings\", \"Networks\") || {}\n    return if networks.key?(NETWORK_NAME)\n\n    network.connect(sandbox.container_id)\n  rescue Docker::Error::NotFoundError\n    # Container no longer exists - sync job will fix the DB state\n    raise Error, \"Sandbox container not found. Please refresh and try again.\"\n  end"
}
```

> AGENT

Fixed! Now when a sandbox has a stale container ID, the terminal open will show a clearer error: "Sandbox container not found. Please refresh and try again."

The `ContainerSyncJob` (runs every 5 minutes) will detect and fix stale container states by marking them as "destroyed". After refreshing, the sandbox will show the correct status instead of appearing to be running when the container is actually gone.

> AGENT

Perfect! The reset task now works correctly. It successfully:

1. **Removed 2 WeTTY containers** (IDs: b54b58c672a2, 6cfe0de23677)
2. **Removed 1 Tailscale container** (ID: 00b8668d5f94)
3. **Cleaned up compose containers, volumes, and networks**

The warnings about `BUILD_VERSION`, `BUILD_GIT_SHA`, etc. are harmless - those variables are only needed when building/deploying, not when cleaning up.

The fix was to use `-q` (quiet mode) instead of `--format "{{.Names}}"` to avoid mise's template engine trying to parse the Go template syntax.

- **Password**: `sandcastle`

```
    46→var createCmd = &cobra.Command{
    47→	Use:   "create <name>",
    48→	Short: "Create a new sandbox",
    49→	Long: `Create a new sandbox.
    50→
    51→Environment variables can set defaults for commonly used flags:
    52→  SANDCASTLE_HOME=1    equivalent to --home
    53→  SANDCASTLE_DATA=.    equivalent to --data (value is the subpath, "." or "1" for root)
    54→  SANDCASTLE_RM=1      equivalent to --rm
    55→
    56→Flags explicitly passed on the command line take precedence over environment variables.`,
    57→	Args: cobra.ExactArgs(1),
    58→	PreRun: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) {
    59→		if !cmd.Flags().Changed("home") && envTruthy("SANDCASTLE_HOME") {
    60→			sandboxHome = true
    61→		}
    62→		if !cmd.Flags().Changed("rm") && envTruthy("SANDCASTLE_RM") {
    63→			sandboxRemove = true
    64→		}
    65→		if !cmd.Flags().Changed("data") {
    66→			if v := os.Getenv("SANDCASTLE_DATA"); v != "" {
    67→				if v == "1" || v == "true" {
    68→					v = "."
    69→				}
    70→				sandboxData = v
    71→			}
    72→		}
    73→	},
    74→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
    75→		client, err := api.NewClient()
    76→		if err != nil {
    77→			return err
    78→		}
    79→		printServer(client)
    80→
    81→		sandbox, err := client.CreateSandbox(api.CreateSandboxRequest{
    82→			Name:       args[0],
    83→			Image:      sandboxImage,
    84→			Persistent: sandboxPersistent,
    85→			Snapshot:   sandboxSnapshot,
    86→			Tailscale:  sandboxTailscale,
    87→			MountHome:  sandboxHome,
    88→			DataPath:   sandboxData,
    89→			Temporary:  sandboxRemove,
    90→		})
    91→		if err != nil {
    92→			return err
    93→		}
    94→
    95→		fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q created.\n", sandbox.Name)
    96→
    97→		// Print active options (use local flags — they reflect what was actually requested)
    98→		if sandboxHome || sandboxData != "" || sandboxPersistent || sandbox.Tailscale || sandboxRemove {
    99→			if sandboxHome {
   100→				fmt.Println("  Home:      mounted (~/ persisted)")
   101→			}
   102→			if sandboxData != "" {
   103→				label := sandboxData
   104→				if label == "." {
   105→					label = "user data root"
   106→				}
   107→				fmt.Printf("  Data:      mounted (%s → /data)\n", label)
   108→			}
   109→			if sandboxPersistent {
   110→				fmt.Println("  Volume:    persistent (/workspace)")
   111→			}
   112→			if sandbox.Tailscale {
   113→				fmt.Println("  Tailscale: enabled")
   114→			}
   115→			if sandboxRemove {
   116→				fmt.Println("  Cleanup:   auto-remove on exit")
   117→			}
   118→		}
   119→
   120→		if sandboxNoConnect {
   121→			return nil
   122→		}
   123→
   124→		info, err := client.ConnectInfo(sandbox.ID)
   125→		if err != nil {
   126→			return err
   127→		}
   128→
   129→		if err := waitForSSH(info.Host, info.Port); err != nil {
   130→			return err
   131→		}
   132→
   133→		sshErr := sshExec(info.Host, info.Port, info.User, "tmux new-session -A -s main")
   134→
   135→		if sandboxRemove {
   136→			// Re-fetch to check if user toggled to "keep" during the session
   137→			current, fetchErr := client.GetSandbox(sandbox.ID)
   138→			if fetchErr == nil && !current.Temporary {
   139→				fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q was set to keep — skipping removal.\n", sandbox.Name)
   140→			} else {
   141→				fmt.Printf("Removing sandbox %q...\n", sandbox.Name)
   142→				if err := client.DestroySandbox(sandbox.ID); err != nil {
   143→					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "Warning: failed to delete sandbox: %v\n", err)
   144→				} else {
   145→					fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q deleted.\n", sandbox.Name)
   146→				}
   147→			}
   148→		}
   149→
   150→		return sshErr
   151→	},
   152→}

   154→var listCmd = &cobra.Command{
   155→	Use:     "list",
   156→	Aliases: []string{"ls"},
   157→	Short:   "List all sandboxes",
   158→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   159→		client, err := api.NewClient()
   160→		if err != nil {
   161→			return err
   162→		}
   163→		printServer(client)
   164→
   165→		sandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()
   166→		if err != nil {
   167→			return err
   168→		}
   169→
   170→		if len(sandboxes) == 0 {
   171→			fmt.Println("No sandboxes.")
   172→			return nil
   173→		}
   174→
   175→		hasRoute := false
   176→		for _, s := range sandboxes {
   177→			if len(s.Routes) > 0 {
   178→				hasRoute = true
   179→				break
   180→			}
   181→		}
   182→
   183→		w := tabwriter.NewWriter(os.Stdout, 0, 0, 2, ' ', 0)
   184→		if hasRoute {
   185→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "NAME\tSTATUS\tPORT\tROUTE\tTAILSCALE IP\tIMAGE")
   186→		} else {
   187→			fmt.Fprintln(w, "NAME\tSTATUS\tPORT\tTAILSCALE IP\tIMAGE")
   188→		}
   189→		for _, s := range sandboxes {
   190→			name := s.Name
   191→			if s.Temporary {
   192→				name += " (temp)"
   193→			}
   194→			tsIP := ""
   195→			if s.TailscaleIP != "" {
   196→				tsIP = s.TailscaleIP
   197→			}
   198→			if hasRoute {
   199→				route := ""
   200→				if len(s.Routes) > 0 {
   201→					parts := make([]string, len(s.Routes))
   202→					for i, r := range s.Routes {
   203→						parts[i] = fmt.Sprintf("%s (:%d)", r.URL, r.Port)
   204→					}
   205→					route = strings.Join(parts, ", ")
   206→				}
   207→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\t%s\t%d\t%s\t%s\t%s\n", name, s.Status, s.SSHPort, route, tsIP, s.Image)
   208→			} else {
   209→				fmt.Fprintf(w, "%s\t%s\t%d\t%s\t%s\n", name, s.Status, s.SSHPort, tsIP, s.Image)
   210→			}
   211→		}
   212→		w.Flush()
   213→		return nil
   214→	},
   215→}

   294→var useCmd = &cobra.Command{
   295→	Use:   "use [name]",
   296→	Short: "Show or set active server/sandbox",
   297→	Long: `Without arguments, shows the current server and active sandbox.
   298→With an argument, switches the active server or sandbox.
   299→
   300→Examples:
   301→  sandcastle use                  # Show current server and sandbox
   302→  sandcastle use my-sandbox       # Set active sandbox
   303→  sandcastle use prod             # Switch to server "prod" (if configured)`,
   304→	Args: cobra.MaximumNArgs(1),
   305→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   306→		cfg, err := config.Load()
   307→		if err != nil {
   308→			return err
   309→		}
   310→
   311→		// No args: list all servers, highlight active
   312→		if len(args) == 0 {
   313→			if len(cfg.Servers) == 0 {
   314→				fmt.Println("No servers configured — run: sandcastle login <url>")
   315→				return nil
   316→			}
   317→
   318→			aliases := make([]string, 0, len(cfg.Servers))
   319→			for alias := range cfg.Servers {
   320→				aliases = append(aliases, alias)
   321→			}
   322→			sort.Strings(aliases)
   323→
   324→			for _, alias := range aliases {
   325→				srv := cfg.Servers[alias]
   326→				if alias == cfg.CurrentServer {
   327→					fmt.Printf("  \033[1m* %-16s\033[0m %s\n", alias, srv.URL)
   328→				} else {
   329→					fmt.Printf("    %-16s %s\n", alias, srv.URL)
   330→				}
   331→			}
   332→
   333→			return nil
   334→		}
   335→
   336→		name := args[0]
   337→
   338→		// Check if name matches a server alias or URL
   339→		if _, ok := cfg.Servers[name]; ok {
   340→			cfg.CurrentServer = name
   341→			if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
   342→				return err
   343→			}
   344→			fmt.Printf("Switched to server %s (%s)\n", name, cfg.Servers[name].URL)
   345→			return nil
   346→		}
   347→
   348→		// Try matching by URL
   349→		for alias, srv := range cfg.Servers {
   350→			if srv.URL == strings.TrimRight(name, "/") {
   351→				cfg.CurrentServer = alias
   352→				if err := config.Save(cfg); err != nil {
   353→					return err
   354→				}
   355→				fmt.Printf("Switched to server %s (%s)\n", alias, srv.URL)
   356→				return nil
   357→			}
   358→		}
   359→
   360→		return fmt.Errorf("server %q not found — run: sandcastle use", name)
   361→	},
   362→}
   363→
   364→var setCmd = &cobra.Command{
   365→	Use:   "set <name> <temp|keep>",
   366→	Short: "Toggle sandbox between temporary and kept",
   367→	Long: `Toggle a sandbox between temporary (auto-remove on exit) and kept.
   368→
   369→  temp   Mark as temporary — will be removed when the CLI session exits
   370→  keep   Mark as kept — will not be auto-removed`,
   371→	Args: cobra.ExactArgs(2),
   372→	RunE: func(cmd *cobra.Command, args []string) error {
   373→		client, err := api.NewClient()
   374→		if err != nil {
   375→			return err
   376→		}
   377→		printServer(client)
   378→
   379→		sandbox, err := findSandboxByName(client, args[0])
   380→		if err != nil {
   381→			return err
   382→		}
   383→
   384→		mode := strings.ToLower(args[1])
   385→		var temp bool
   386→		switch mode {
   387→		case "temp":
   388→			temp = true
   389→		case "keep":
   390→			temp = false
   391→		default:
   392→			return fmt.Errorf("unknown mode %q: use \"temp\" or \"keep\"", args[1])
   393→		}
   394→
   395→		sandbox, err = client.UpdateSandbox(sandbox.ID, api.UpdateSandboxRequest{Temporary: &temp})
   396→		if err != nil {
   397→			return err
   398→		}
   399→
   400→		if temp {
   401→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q set to temporary (will be removed on exit).\n", sandbox.Name)
   402→		} else {
   403→			fmt.Printf("Sandbox %q set to keep (will not be removed on exit).\n", sandbox.Name)
   404→		}
   405→		return nil
   406→	},
   407→}
   408→
   409→func envTruthy(key string) bool {
   410→	v := strings.ToLower(os.Getenv(key))
   411→	return v == "1" || v == "true" || v == "yes"
   412→}
   413→
   414→func findSandboxByName(client *api.Client, name string) (*api.Sandbox, error) {
   415→	sandboxes, err := client.ListSandboxes()
   416→	if err != nil {
   417→		return nil, err
   418→	}
   419→	for _, s := range sandboxes {
   420→		if s.Name == name {
   421→			return &s, nil
   422→		}
   423→	}
   424→	return nil, fmt.Errorf("sandbox %q not found", name)
   425→}
   426→
```

```json
{
  "command": "gh issue create --title \"Add web UI form to create sandboxes\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Feature Request\n\nAdd a web form on the dashboard to create sandboxes with all the options available in the `sandcastle create` CLI command.\n\n### Current Behavior\n\nUsers must use the CLI to create sandboxes:\n```bash\nsandcastle create my-dev\n```\n\n### Desired Behavior\n\nAdd a \"Create Sandbox\" button/form on the dashboard that includes all CLI options:\n\n**Form Fields:**\n\n1. **Name** (required, text input)\n   - Sandbox name identifier\n   - Validation: lowercase, alphanumeric, hyphens\n\n2. **Image** (text input, default: `ghcr.io/thieso2/sandcastle-sandbox:latest`)\n   - Container image to use\n   - Show default value as placeholder\n\n3. **Persistent Volume** (checkbox, default: unchecked)\n   - Enable persistent `/workspace` volume\n   - Label: \"Enable persistent volume\"\n\n4. **Snapshot** (select/dropdown, optional)\n   - Create from existing snapshot\n   - List available snapshots for current user\n   - Option: \"None (fresh sandbox)\"\n\n5. **Tailscale** (checkbox, default: unchecked)\n   - Connect to Tailscale network\n   - Label: \"Connect to Tailscale\"\n   - Only show if user has Tailscale enabled\n\n6. **Mount Home** (checkbox, default: unchecked)\n   - Mount persistent home directory\n   - Label: \"Mount persistent home directory\"\n   - Description: \"Your home directory (~/) will persist across sandboxes\"\n\n7. **Data Path** (text input, optional)\n   - Mount user data directory to `/data`\n   - Placeholder: \"e.g., . (all user data) or projects/myapp\"\n   - Description: \"Mount user data directory (or subpath) to /data\"\n\n8. **Temporary** (checkbox, default: unchecked)\n   - Delete sandbox on exit\n   - Label: \"Temporary (auto-remove on exit)\"\n   - Show warning icon/text\n\n### Implementation Notes\n\n- Use the same form validation as the CLI\n- Show helpful descriptions/tooltips for each option\n- Consider a \"Quick Create\" vs \"Advanced Options\" toggle to hide less-used fields\n- After creation, redirect to sandbox detail page or offer \"Connect\" button\n- The `--no-connect` flag isn't needed for web UI (user clicks connect manually)\n\n### Reference\n\nCLI implementation: `vendor/sandcastle-cli/cmd/sandbox.go` lines 35-43, 81-90\nEOF\n)\"",
  "description": "Create GitHub issue for sandbox creation form"
}
```

> AGENT

The issue is that Traefik isn't running in the local deployment, but it's required to route terminal requests to WeTTY containers. The local docker-compose file doesn't include Traefik.

You have two options:

**Option 1: Add Traefik to local deployment** (recommended for full feature testing)

Would you like me to add Traefik to `docker-compose.local.yml`?

**Option 2: Use direct SSH instead of web terminal**

For local development, you can connect via SSH directly:
```bash
ssh -p <ssh_port> <username>@localhost
```

Check the sandbox SSH port with:
```bash
sandcastle list
```

Which approach would you prefer?

> AGENT

Done! I've added Traefik to the local deployment. Here's what changed:

**Added services:**
- **traefik** - Reverse proxy on ports 8080 (HTTP) and 8443 (HTTPS)
- **init-traefik** - Sets up initial Traefik configuration

**Updates:**
- Removed direct port binding from web service (now accessed via Traefik)
- Shared `traefik-data` volume between Traefik and web for dynamic configs
- Updated mise task to show both Traefik URLs

**To apply the changes:**

```bash
mise run deploy:local:reset   # Clean up old deployment
mise run deploy:local          # Start with Traefik
```

**Access the app:**
- **https://localhost:8443** - Main app with terminal support (HTTPS, self-signed cert)
- **http://localhost:8080** - Main app (HTTP)

The web terminal should now work at `https://localhost:8443/terminal/{id}/wetty` 🎉

> AGENT

Found the issue! Rails can't write to the Traefik directory due to permission denied. The directory is owned by root but Rails runs as UID 1000. Let me fix the init-traefik service:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01UB1tqzoNGJN8qKGgimKfwJ
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/docker-compose.local.yml",
  "old_string": "      - sh\n      - -c\n      - |\n        mkdir -p /data/dynamic /data/certs\n        cat > /data/dynamic/rails.yml << 'EOFCONFIG'",
  "new_string": "      - sh\n      - -c\n      - |\n        mkdir -p /data/dynamic /data/certs\n        chown -R 1000:1000 /data\n        cat > /data/dynamic/rails.yml << 'EOFCONFIG'"
}
```

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_01UB1tqzoNGJN8qKGgimKfwJ
```
The file /Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/docker-compose.local.yml has been updated successfully.
```

```bash
mise run deploy:local
```

> AGENT

Looking at our conversation history, here's what happened:

**What you asked:** "create a GH issue 'i want to create a sandcastle' on the website..."

**What I did:** Created issue #13 with a detailed feature request

**What I didn't do:** Implement the feature, create a branch, or create a PR

## Why I didn't create a PR/branch:

1. **You only asked for an issue** - Your request was specifically to "create a GH issue", not to implement the feature
2. **Issue creation ≠ implementation** - Creating an issue is just documenting a feature request for future work
3. **No implementation request** - You didn't ask me to build the feature, just to track it

## If you want me to implement it:

If you'd like me to actually build this feature, just ask! I would then:
1. Create a new branch (e.g., `feat/sandbox-create-form`)
2. Implement the web form with all the CLI fields
3. Commit the changes
4. Create a PR linking to issue #13

**Would you like me to implement this feature now?** I can create the sandbox creation form on the dashboard with all the fields from the CLI command.

```
name: Claude Code

on:
  issue_comment:
    types: [created]
  pull_request_review_comment:
    types: [created]
  issues:
    types: [opened, assigned]
  pull_request_review:
    types: [submitted]

jobs:
  claude:
    if: |
      (github.event_name == 'issue_comment' && contains(github.event.comment.body, '@claude')) ||
      (github.event_name == 'pull_request_review_comment' && contains(github.event.comment.body, '@claude')) ||
      (github.event_name == 'pull_request_review' && contains(github.event.review.body, '@claude')) ||
      (github.event_name == 'issues' && (contains(github.event.issue.body, '@claude') || contains(github.event.issue.title, '@claude')))
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
      pull-requests: read
      issues: read
      id-token: write
      actions: read # Required for Claude to read CI results on PRs
    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          fetch-depth: 1

      - name: Run Claude Code
        id: claude
        uses: anthropics/claude-code-action@v1
        with:
          claude_code_oauth_token: ${{ secrets.CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN }}

          # This is an optional setting that allows Claude to read CI results on PRs
          additional_permissions: |
            actions: read

          # Optional: Give a custom prompt to Claude. If this is not specified, Claude will perform the instructions specified in the comment that tagged it.
          # prompt: 'Update the pull request description to include a summary of changes.'

          # Optional: Add claude_args to customize behavior and configuration
          # See https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code-action/blob/main/docs/usage.md
          # or https://code.claude.com/docs/en/cli-reference for available options
          # claude_args: '--allowed-tools Bash(gh pr:*)'
```

## Analysis: Why Claude Didn't Create a Branch or PR

I found the issue! Looking at the GitHub Action workflow and Claude's comments:

### What Happened

1. **Claude DID the work** - It implemented the complete sandbox creation form
2. **Claude staged the changes** - All files were ready to commit
3. **Claude COULDN'T commit or push** - Permission denied on git commands

### Root Cause: Permission Restrictions

The workflow file `.github/workflows/claude.yml` has restricted permissions:

```yaml
permissions:
  contents: read        # ❌ Read-only! Should be "write"
  pull-requests: read   # ❌ Read-only! Should be "write"  
  issues: read
  id-token: write
  actions: read
```

**With `contents: read`, Claude can:**
- ✅ Read repository code
- ✅ Comment on issues
- ❌ Create branches
- ❌ Commit changes
- ❌ Push to repository
- ❌ Create pull requests

### Evidence from Logs

From Claude's last comment on issue #13:
> "I'm unable to commit and push due to permission restrictions on git commands."

And from the second run:
> "The previous work mentioned in the earlier comment was **not actually committed or pushed**... The previous Claude response indicated that work was completed and staged, but it appears the final git commit and push were never executed."

### The Fix

Update `.github/workflows/claude.yml` to grant write permissions:

```yaml
permissions:
  contents: write        # ✅ Allows git commits and pushes
  pull-requests: write   # ✅ Allows creating PRs
  issues: write          # ✅ Allows updating issues
  id-token: write
  actions: read
```

**Would you like me to update the workflow file with the correct permissions?**
