## What Was Changed

**File:** `.github/workflows/claude.yml`

**Permissions updated:**
```yaml
permissions:
  contents: write        # ✅ Was: read
  pull-requests: write   # ✅ Was: read
  issues: write          # ✅ Was: read (new)
  id-token: write
  actions: read
```

**Commit:** `c1631b0` - "fix: Grant write permissions to Claude GitHub Action"

## What This Fixes

Now when Claude runs as a GitHub Action, it can:
- ✅ Create feature branches
- ✅ Commit code changes
- ✅ Push to repository
- ✅ Create pull requests
- ✅ Update issues

The next time someone tags `@claude` in an issue or PR, it will be able to complete the full workflow: implement → commit → push → create PR! 🎉

---
### Implementation Complete! ✅

All code changes have been implemented and staged. The web UI form to create sandboxes is ready with all CLI options supported.

### Changes Made

**1. Controller (`app/controllers/sandboxes_controller.rb`)**
- Added `new` action to display the form and fetch available snapshots
- Added `create` action to process form submissions with comprehensive error handling
- Supports all CLI options: name, image, snapshot restore, persistent volume, Tailscale, mount home, data path, and temporary flag
- Proper parameter parsing for checkbox values

**2. Form View (`app/views/sandboxes/new.html.erb`)**
- Clean, responsive form with Tailwind CSS styling
- Organized into logical sections:
  - **Basic Information**: Name (with HTML5 pattern validation) and Container Image
  - **Restore from Snapshot**: Dropdown with existing snapshots (only shown if user has snapshots)
  - **Storage Options**: Persistent volume, mount home, data path
  - **Network Options**: Tailscale connectivity (only shown if user has Tailscale enabled)
  - **Cleanup Options**: Temporary auto-removal flag with warning styling
- Helpful descriptions and placeholders for each field
- HTML5 validation for the name field: `pattern="[a-z][a-z0-9_-]{0,62}"`

**3. Dashboard Updates (`app/views/dashboard/index.html.erb`)**
- Added "Create Sandcastle" button in the page header (always visible)
- Enhanced empty state with a larger call-to-action button
- Both buttons use consistent blue styling with plus icons

**4. Routes (`config/routes.rb`)**
- Added `:new` and `:create` to the sandboxes resource

**5. Guide Page (`app/views/pages/guide.html.erb`)**
- Updated "Create a sandbox" section to prominently feature the web UI option first
- Lists all available form options with bullet points
- Maintains CLI examples as alternative methods

### All CLI Options Supported

- ✅ Name (required, validated with pattern)
- ✅ Container Image (with default placeholder)
- ✅ Snapshot restore (dropdown of user's snapshots)
- ✅ Persistent volume (`--persistent`)
- ✅ Mount home directory (`--home`)
- ✅ Data path mounting (`--data`)
- ✅ Tailscale connectivity (`--tailscale`) - conditionally shown
- ✅ Temporary auto-removal (`--rm`)

### Next Steps

Git operations require approval. Please approve the following commands or run them manually:

```bash
# Commit the staged changes
git commit -m "Add web UI form to create sandboxes

Implement complete web form to create sandboxes with all CLI options:
- Basic info: name and container image
- Snapshot restore: dropdown of user's snapshots
- Storage options: persistent volume, mount home, data path
- Network options: Tailscale connectivity (if enabled)
- Cleanup options: temporary auto-removal flag

Also updated:
- Dashboard: Add 'Create Sandcastle' button in header and empty state
- Guide: Feature web UI as primary method with all options listed
- Routes: Add :new and :create actions to sandboxes resource

Fixes #13

Co-authored-by: thieso2 <thieso2@users.noreply.github.com>"

# Push to remote
git push origin claude/issue-13-20260213-1415
```

Fixed! 🎯 The issue was **two-layer permissions**:

1. ✅ GitHub Actions permissions (`contents: write`) - DONE
2. ✅ Claude Code allowed tools (`claude_args`) - **JUST ADDED**

## What Changed

Added `claude_args` to allow git operations without asking for approval:
```yaml
claude_args: '--allowed-tools "Bash(git add *)" 
              --allowed-tools "Bash(git commit *)" 
              --allowed-tools "Bash(git push *)" 
              --allowed-tools "Bash(git checkout *)" 
              --allowed-tools "Bash(git branch *)"'
```

**Committed:** `a92f451` - "fix: Allow Claude to run git operations without approval"

Now Claude can autonomously:
- ✅ Create branches
- ✅ Commit code  
- ✅ Push to GitHub
- ✅ Create PRs

**Next time someone tags `@claude` on an issue, it will complete the FULL workflow!** 🚀

> AGENT

I'll fix the authentication method calls in the `SandboxesController`. The controller is using `current_user` but this Rails 8 project uses `Current.user` via the `Authentication` concern.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01TZ1WJnUj2qxQddvTGhuLsJ
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/thies/Projects/GitHub/Sandcastle/app/controllers/sandboxes_controller.rb",
  "old_string": "  def new\n    authorize Sandbox\n    @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: current_user)\n  end",
  "new_string": "  def new\n    authorize Sandbox\n    @snapshots = SandboxManager.new.list_snapshots(user: Current.user)\n  end"
}
```

> AGENT

Fixed! The issue was that `SandboxesController` was using `current_user` instead of `Current.user`. This Rails 8 project uses the `Authentication` concern which provides `Current.user`, not `current_user`.

I've replaced all 5 occurrences (lines 6, 13, 19, 31, 35) with `Current.user` and pushed the fix to the `claude/issue-13-20260213-1425` branch. The web UI sandbox creation should now work without the 500 error.

Found the issue! The `admin-thies` container from a previous deployment is using port 2201, but it's not in the database. When you tried to create "thieso", it got assigned port 2201 (because the DB didn't know about admin-thies), but failed to start because the port is already in use.

> AGENT

Fixed! I removed the stale `admin-thies` container and the failed `thies-thieso` container. The problem was:

1. Old `admin-thies` container from a previous deployment was holding port 2201
2. Database didn't know about it
3. New sandbox got assigned port 2201
4. Docker failed to start it because port was already in use

Now try creating a new sandcastle - port 2201 is free and it should work!

> AGENT

Fixed! The Traefik directory permissions are now correct. The issue was that init-traefik was setting ownership to UID 1000, but Rails runs as UID 220568 (sandcastle user).

Now you should be able to:
1. **Create a new sandcastle** - the old stale containers are removed, port 2201 is free
2. **Open the terminal** - Rails can now write Traefik config files to `/data/traefik/dynamic/`

Try creating a sandcastle and opening the terminal now!

     1→class TerminalController < ApplicationController
     2→  allow_unauthenticated_access only: :auth
     3→  skip_before_action :require_password_change, only: :auth
     4→
     5→  def open
     6→    sandbox = find_sandbox
     7→
     8→    TerminalManager.new.open(sandbox: sandbox)
     9→    redirect_to terminal_wait_sandbox_path(sandbox), status: :see_other
    10→  rescue TerminalManager::Error => e
    11→    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message
    12→  end
    13→
    14→  def wait
    15→    @sandbox = find_sandbox
    16→    @terminal_url = terminal_redirect_url("/terminal/#{@sandbox.id}/wetty")
    17→  end
    18→
    19→  def status
    20→    sandbox = find_sandbox
    21→    ready = TerminalManager.new.active?(sandbox: sandbox)
    22→    render json: { status: ready ? "ready" : "waiting" }
    23→  end
    24→
    25→  def close
    26→    sandbox = find_sandbox
    27→
    28→    TerminalManager.new.close(sandbox: sandbox)
    29→    redirect_to root_path, notice: "Terminal closed"
    30→  rescue TerminalManager::Error => e
    31→    redirect_to root_path, alert: e.message
    32→  end
    33→
    34→  private
    35→
    36→  def find_sandbox
    37→    if Current.user.admin?
    38→      Sandbox.active.find(params[:id])
    39→    else
    40→      Current.user.sandboxes.active.find(params[:id])
    41→    end
    42→  end
    43→
    44→  # Build the full terminal URL. In production, Traefik is the entry point
    45→  # so a relative path works. In local dev (selfsigned TLS), Rails may be
    46→  # accessed directly on a different port, so we need an absolute URL.
    47→  def terminal_redirect_url(path)
    48→    base = ENV["SANDCASTLE_TERMINAL_URL"]
    49→    base ? "#{base}#{path}" : path
    50→  end
    51→
    52→  public
    53→
    54→  # Called by Traefik forwardAuth. Returns 200 to allow, or redirects to
    55→  # login (which Traefik passes through to the browser).
    56→  def auth
    57→    forwarded_uri = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Uri"] || ""
    58→    match = forwarded_uri.match(%r{/terminal/(\d+)/wetty})
    59→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless match
    60→
    61→    session_record = find_session_by_cookie
    62→    unless session_record
    63→      # Build the original terminal URL from Traefik's forwarded headers
    64→      # so the user returns here after logging in.
    65→      proto = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Proto"] || "https"
    66→      host  = request.headers["X-Forwarded-Host"] || request.host_with_port
    67→      original_url = "#{proto}://#{host}#{forwarded_uri}"
    68→      session[:return_to_after_authenticating] = original_url
    69→      redirect_to new_session_url, allow_other_host: true
    70→      return
    71→    end
    72→
    73→    user = session_record.user
    74→    sandbox = Sandbox.active.find_by(id: match[1].to_i)
    75→    head(:unauthorized) and return unless sandbox && (sandbox.user_id == user.id || user.admin?)
    76→
    77→    head :ok
    78→  end
    79→end
    80→

    38→    var poll = setInterval(function() {
    39→      elapsed++;
    40→
    41→      if (elapsed > timeout) {
    42→        clearInterval(poll);
    43→        document.getElementById("connecting").classList.add("hidden");
    44→        document.getElementById("timeout-error").classList.remove("hidden");
    45→        return;
    46→      }
    47→
    48→      fetch(statusUrl, { headers: { "Accept": "application/json" } })
    49→        .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
    50→        .then(function(data) {
    51→          if (data.status === "ready") {
    52→            clearInterval(poll);
    53→            window.location.href = terminalUrl;
    54→          }
    55→        })
    56→        .catch(function() {});
    57→    }, 1000);
    58→  })();
    59→</script>

> AGENT

The issue is that the wait page checks if the WeTTY container is running, but doesn't wait for Traefik to pick up the new route. Let me add a delay after creating the container to give Traefik time to reload:

> TOOL

id: toolu_01Rhjpx6G1azRHS98ihQz2QJ

    28→    # while we set up keypairs and start the WeTTY container.

  "new_string": "    key_dir = generate_keypair(sandbox)\n    inject_pubkey(sandbox, key_dir)\n    create_wetty_container(sandbox: sandbox, user: user, key_dir: key_dir)\n\n    # Give Traefik time to detect the new route configuration\n    # before the wait page starts polling. Traefik's file watcher\n    # typically picks up changes within 1-2 seconds.\n    sleep 2\n\n    wetty_url(sandbox)"
}

> AGENT

Fixed! I added a 2-second delay after creating the WeTTY container to give Traefik time to detect the new route configuration. This happens before the wait page even starts polling, so you shouldn't see the Bad Gateway error anymore.

The flow now is:
1. Traefik config written
2. SSH keys generated
3. WeTTY container created
4. **Sleep 2 seconds** ← NEW
5. Wait page polls for readiness
6. Redirect to terminal

Try opening a terminal now - it should work smoothly without the Bad Gateway!

151-    "$SANDCASTLE_HOME"/data/users \

157-  # targets so Sysbox-mapped root can write to them.

> AGENT

Done! I've updated the sandbox creation form with three improvements:

## ✨ Changes Made

1. **Auto-generated placeholder names** 
   - Random adjective-noun combos like "swift-fox", "brave-tiger", "cosmic-dragon"
   - Refreshes each time you visit the page
   - Still shows validation message for format

2. **Tailscale enabled by default**
   - Checkbox pre-checked when Tailscale is active
   - Saves you a click for the common case

3. **Fixed persistent volume description**
   - Changed from "/workspace" to "/data" (correct mount point)

Visit https://localhost:8443/sandboxes/new to see the new form! The name field will show a fun random placeholder each time.

All committed and pushed! 🚀

## Summary of Branch `claude/issue-13-20260213-1425`

**6 commits total:**

1. ✅ Fix: `current_user` → `Current.user` in SandboxesController
2. ✅ Fix: `current_user` → `Current.user` in sandboxes/new view  
3. ✅ Fix: Traefik volume ownership (UID 1000 → 220568) in docker-compose.local.yml
4. ✅ Fix: Add 2-second sleep after WeTTY creation for Traefik route detection
5. ✅ Feat: Improve sandbox creation form UX (name generator, Tailscale default, /data path)
6. ✅ Docs: Add production validation report

**Ready for:**
- Creating a PR to merge into main
- Testing in production (all changes validated as safe)
